Abstract: This preliminary technical report presents a framework for sign language video synthesis using a loss-guided multi-expert Generative Adversarial Network (GAN) to enhance communication for individuals with hearing impairments. Three specialized discriminators -- global, hand, and head -- each guide a corresponding expert branch in the generator toward a distinct visual region, enabling implicit feature specialization without explicit diversity losses. To stabilize this multi-discriminator system, whose early-phase training otherwise exhibits chaotic dynamics, we introduce a United Loss consensus mechanism that regularizes each discriminator toward the ensemble average at a 10% weight. Each branch further adopts a dual-pathway convolutional-transformer design with learnable AdaptiveFeatureFusion, balancing the stability of convolutions against the detail of windowed self-attention. The generator is trained using an alternating three-mode schedule (discriminator, holistic generation, branch-specialized generation). On a custom 156GB dataset with a filtered test set that removes easy and repetitive samples, our 0.2B-parameter variant achieves 29.8 PSNR and the 1.3B-parameter variant achieves 30.7 PSNR, with inference VRAM footprints of 1.5 GB and 8 GB respectively, enabling deployment on consumer-grade hardware. Full ablation studies remain ongoing due to the 2-3 month training cycle on a single GPU. The system was showcased at the 2025 Hong Kong Frontier Technology Summit.

</details>


### [72] [Reconstructing Historical Manuscripts through MSI: The Potential of Contrast in Assessing Image Quality and Legibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13381)
*Anna Breger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究探讨了基于对比度的图像质量指标在评估多光谱成像重建手稿图像质量和可读性方面的适用性。通过两个公开数据集的实验，对比了专家评分和全参考图像质量度量基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史手稿图像的数字修复旨在提高可读性同时保护文化遗产文献的真实性。然而，手稿质量评估具有挑战性，因为可读性通常是主观的，且专家注释稀缺。

Method: 使用公开数据集进行两个实验，邀请专家提供主观质量评分，并使用全参考图像质量度量作为参考评估，对比分析了多种基于对比度的图像质量指标。

Result: 势对比度(Potential Contrast)与专家评分达到最高相关性，而对比度-噪声比(Contrast-to-Noise Ratio)与全参考质量度量表现最强一致。总体而言，基于对比度的度量始终优于通用图像质量度量。

Conclusion: 基于对比度的度量展现出作为手稿可读性和重建质量客观指标的潜力，有望为文化遗产文献的数字化修复质量评估提供有效手段。

Abstract: Digital restoration of historical manuscript images aims to improve readability while preserving the authenticity of cultural heritage documents. However, evaluating quality of restored manuscripts remains challenging, where readability is often subjective and expert annotations are scarce. This study investigates the suitability of contrast-based image quality measures to assess quality and legibility of reconstructed manuscript images from multi-spectral imaging. Two experiments were conducted with publicly-available data sets, facilitating manual quality scores by experts and full-reference image quality measures as reference evaluations. The results show that potential contrast achieves the highest correlation with expert ratings, while contrast-to-noise ratio demonstrates the strongest agreement with full-reference quality measures. Overall, contrast-based measures consistently outperform general image quality measures, demonstrating their potential as objective indicators of manuscript legibility and reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [73] [When Is a Task Vector Enough? An Empirical Theory of Implicit Multimodal ICL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13385)
*Jiaqian Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了"选择-实现假说"(Selection-Realization Hypothesis)，解释隐式多模态上下文学习(M-ICL)如何运作。研究发现静态任务向量的成功取决于演示诱导的变化在查询间共享程度，而更复杂的干预在处理查询特定或分布式结构时才变得必要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式多模态上下文学习方法在干预方式上存在显著差异（从静态任务向量到查询条件变换和注意力路由），但尚不清楚对于给定任务需要多少额外复杂性。本文旨在建立一个统一的经验理论来指导方法选择。

Method: 通过受控多模态任务进行评估，使查询依赖性变化而保持底层任务原语和提示格式不变。通过对比正确演示与匹配的逆向示例，测量显式M-ICL的结构并预测干预行为。实验涵盖自然VQA基准测试。

Result: 静态任务向量的成功与演示诱导变化在查询间的共享程度密切相关。当显式M-ICL包含查询特定或分布式结构时，局部加性偏移无法恢复，需要更复杂的干预。这些关系可扩展到自然VQA基准测试，并支持无需访问测试性能的成本感知方法选择。

Conclusion: 研究提供了一个统一的经验理论，解释何时可将演示压缩为任务向量，何时需要更表达性的干预。为实际应用中的成本感知方法选择提供了指导。

Abstract: Implicit multimodal in-context learning compresses demonstrations into internal interventions, ranging from static task vectors to query-conditioned transformations and attention routing. Despite their common goal, these methods differ substantially in how the intervention depends on the query and where it modifies the model, leaving unclear which additional complexity is necessary for a given task. We propose the Selection--Realization Hypothesis. It views demonstrations as inducing a compact family of internal changes from which the query selects, while the model's computation constrains how the selected change can be implemented. We evaluate this account using controlled multimodal tasks in which query dependence varies without changing the underlying task primitives or prompt format. By contrasting correct demonstrations with matched counterfactuals, we measure the structure of explicit M-ICL and test whether it predicts intervention behavior. We find that the success of a static task vector is closely tied to how much of the demonstration-induced change is shared across queries. Additional intervention complexity becomes useful when explicit M-ICL contains query-specific or distributed structure that a local additive shift cannot recover. These relationships extend to natural VQA benchmarks and support cost-aware method selection without access to test performance. Our results provide a unified empirical theory of when demonstrations can be compressed into a task vector and when a more expressive intervention is warranted.

</details>


### [74] [Context-Matched Distillation: Teacher Causality for Autoregressive Video Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13391)
*Hmrishav Bandyopadhyay,Xuanchi Ren,Zijian Huang,Jay Zhangjie Wu,Tianshi Cao,Ruilong Li,Bryan Chu,Sanja Fidler,Yi-Zhe Song,Zian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Context-Matched Distillation (CMD)是一种用于自回归视频生成的因果蒸馏框架，通过将双向教师替换为因果教师来解决蒸馏过程中信息不匹配的问题，使学生能够在每帧生成时只使用历史信息和控制信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频分布匹配蒸馏(DMD)管道使用双向教师评估完整片段，导致学生的目标帧监督可能依赖于生成时不可用的未来帧和控制信号，造成教师监督与学生因果信息集之间的不对齐。

Method: CMD提出三个关键技术：1)因果教师用因果评分替代双向全片段评分；2)前缀评分(Prefix Scoring)使用缓存的学生生成前缀来评估每个目标；3)前缀腐败(Prefix Corruption)扰动训练早期不可靠的前缀。这些方法确保教师监督与学生实际生成时的信息边界一致。

Result: CMD在短视频和长视频基准测试中实现了自回归方法的最佳综合性能，并且显著提高了对时变相机控制的遵循能力。

Conclusion: CMD通过建立统一的因果 formulation，能够自然扩展到帧级和分块级生成、长视频蒸馏和相机条件蒸馏，为自回归视频生成提供了一种简单而有效的蒸馏范式。

Abstract: Interactive autoregressive video generation demands both low-latency rollouts and precise online control. Few-step distillation accelerates generation by reducing denoising steps, while online control imposes a causal constraint: frames and blocks should depend on history and controls available during generation. Existing video distribution matching distillation (DMD) pipelines, however, often supervise causal few-step students using bidirectional teachers that score complete clips. The score for a target can therefore depend on future frames and controls that were unavailable when the student generated it, misaligning teacher supervision with the student's causal information set. We introduce Context-Matched Distillation (CMD), a causal DMD framework that aligns teacher supervision with the information available when each target is generated. CMD replaces bidirectional full-clip scoring with a causal teacher that evaluates each target without access to future frames or controls. The same causal teacher initializes the few-step student, establishing a consistent causal formulation across teacher training, student distillation, and inference. Beyond aligning the temporal information boundary, Prefix Scoring matches supervision to the student's realized rollout context by evaluating each target under the cached student-generated prefix that produced it. Prefix Corruption further stabilizes training by perturbing unreliable prefixes produced early in training while preserving this target-context alignment. With a simple causal formulation, CMD naturally extends to frame-wise and chunk-wise generation, long video distillation, and camera-conditioned distillation. Experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art aggregate performance among autoregressive methods on both short- and long-video benchmarks, together with substantially improved adherence to time-varying camera controls.

</details>


### [75] [StreamTTT: Reconciling Real-Time Perception and Long-Term Memory in Streaming VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13416)
*Joya Chen,Zeyun Zhong,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamTTT通过将长程历史写入注意力上下文外在线更新的快速权重，解决流式视觉语言模型在实时感知与长期记忆之间的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前流式VLMs在实时感知与长期记忆之间存在权衡：缩短上下文可增强当前场景感知但损害长程回忆能力。

Method: 提出StreamTTT方法：1)将长程历史写入在线更新的快速权重（fast weights）；2)保留短期滑窗key-value缓存专用于近期证据；3)联合训练离线长视频QA和新构建的实时QA语料库。

Result: 在OVO-Bench上，StreamTTT-4B比SimpleStream-4B实时感知提升1.4分，向后追溯提升3.7分；在StreamingBench的RTVU子集上与更大的SimpleStream-8B相当。

Conclusion: StreamTTT通过快速权重机制有效缓解了注意力稀释问题，在保持较小参数量的同时实现了实时感知与长程记忆的平衡。

Abstract: Humans effortlessly perceive the present while remembering the past, yet streaming VLMs often trade off real-time perception against long-term memory. Prior work shows that shortening the context can sharpen current-scene perception at the expense of long-range recall. To reconcile these abilities, we introduce StreamTTT, which writes long-range history into online-updated fast weights outside the attention context. This leaves a short sliding key-value cache dedicated to recent evidence, mitigating attention dilution. We train StreamTTT jointly on offline long-video QA and a newly constructed real-time QA corpus. On OVO-Bench, StreamTTT-4B outperforms SimpleStream-4B by 1.4 points in real-time perception and 3.7 points in backward tracing. It also remains competitive with the larger SimpleStream-8B on the Real-Time Visual Understanding (RTVU) subset of StreamingBench. Our code will be released.

</details>


### [76] [Edit2TikZ: A Comprehensive and Challenging Benchmark for Scientific Figure Editing with TikZ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13441)
*Zongyun Zhang,Jiacheng Ruan,Xian Gao,Ruizhu Zhou,Lingcheng Meng,Lining Hu,Ting Liu,Yuzhuo Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Edit2TikZ是一个用于评估多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)在科学图表编辑任务中性能的基准测试平台，解决了现有TikZ基准仅关注图形重建而忽视指令引导编辑的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态大语言模型在视觉理解和图形代码生成方面展现出潜力，但通过代码编辑科学图表更具挑战性：模型需要共同恢复视觉结构、定位请求的更改、生成可编译代码并保留所有无关内容。现有TikZ基准主要关注图形重建和生成，很少系统评估指令引导的科学图表编辑与可编译代码。

Method: 构建了包含1,548个多样化高质量样本的Edit2TikZ基准测试，结合了真实世界和受控的合成编辑案例，支持文本和视觉定位请求，并包含多步骤编辑，每个编辑都有步骤级注释。同时构建了人类对齐的评估框架来衡量请求的编辑是否完成以及无关内容是否被保留。

Result: 对14个主流MLLMs进行评估，发现当前系统仍然不可靠：专利模型平均编译成功率仅为75%，在图形恢复和编辑正确性方面仍然受限，而低于9B的紧凑模型在指令遵循和完整图形生成方面更加困难。在Qwen3.5-4B上，这种训练将编译成功率从45.35%提高到83.40%，并在提出的评估指标上平均提高18.7分。

Conclusion: 提出了Edit2TikZ基准和人类对齐评估框架，为科学图表编辑任务建立了全面评估标准。通过TikZEditMix训练集和重建-编辑课程学习，显著提升了紧凑模型的性能，为该领域未来研究提供了重要基础。

Abstract: Although multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown substantial potential in visual understanding and graphic code generation, editing scientific figures through code presents a greater challenge: a model must jointly recover visual structure, ground the requested change, generate compilable code, and preserve all unrelated content. While existing TikZ benchmarks mainly focus on figure reconstruction and generation, few systematically evaluate instruction-guided scientific figure editing with compilable code. We introduce Edit2TikZ, a comprehensive benchmark for scientific figure editing tasks, featuring 1,548 diverse and high-quality samples. Edit2TikZ combines real-world and controlled synthetic edit cases, supports both textual and visual localization request, and contains multi-step editing, each with step-level annotations. We further construct a human-aligned evaluation framework to measure whether a requested edit is completed while irrelevant content is preserved. Utilizing Edit2TikZ, we evaluate 14 mainstream MLLMs and find that current systems remain unreliable: on average, proprietary models achieve a compilation success rate of merely 75% and remain limited in both figure restoration and edit correctness, while compact models below 9B struggle further with instruction following and complete figure generation. Therefore, we build a mixed training set TikZEditMix and adopt reconstruction-then-editing curriculum learning for compact models. On Qwen3.5-4B, this training improves the compilation success rate from 45.35% to 83.40% and yields an average improvement of 18.7 points across our proposed evaluation metrics. The code and data will be released at https://github.com/Solunny/Edit2TikZ.

</details>


### [77] [UniTexture: Cross-Task Universal Adversarial Textures for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13453)
*Yukun Dai,Mingzhe Dai,Tianshi Wang,Fengling Li,Jingjing Li,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出UniTexture，一种跨任务通用对抗纹理攻击，通过单个纹理化3D物体诱导VLA在多任务中的动作预测产生针对性偏差，利用可微渲染器优化共享纹理来操控机器人动作，攻击可迁移到不同模型和环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人策略攻击通常针对单一任务或指令优化，而多任务VLA的跨任务漏洞尚未被充分探索。该研究旨在发现并利用多任务VLA模型在跨任务层面的安全漏洞。

Method: 提出UniTexture方法，使用可微渲染器将策略动作输出的梯度反向传播到表面纹理参数，针对动作空间目标联合优化共享纹理，使其在不同任务、指令、状态和视角下产生针对攻击者定义目标的动作偏移，而无需为每个任务单独优化纹理。

Result: UniTexture将平均任务成功率从90.0%（良性条件）降低到48.4%（攻击条件），成功诱导目标对齐的动作偏移，并展现出跨模型和跨环境的迁移能力，无需重新优化即可在不同环境中生效。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了多任务VLA模型存在跨任务共享的漏洞，攻击者可通过单一对抗纹理实现对多种任务的攻击，且该攻击具有跨模型和跨环境的迁移能力，这对VLA机器人系统的安全性提出了重大挑战。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as generalist robotic policies capable of following diverse language instructions and performing a wide range of manipulation tasks. However, their direct control over embodied agents also exposes them to adversarial interference that may cause unsafe physical behaviors. Existing attacks on robotic policies are typically optimized for a single task or instruction, leaving the cross-task vulnerabilities of multitask VLAs largely unexplored. We introduce UniTexture, a cross-task universal adversarial texture attack that uses a single textured 3D object to induce targeted deviations in VLA action predictions across multiple tasks. UniTexture backpropagates gradients from the policy's action outputs to surface texture parameters through a differentiable renderer. It jointly optimizes the shared texture over a distribution of tasks, instructions, states, and viewpoints using a targeted action-space objective, steering predicted actions toward attacker-defined targets without optimizing a separate texture for each task. We evaluate UniTexture on OpenVLA and $π_{{0.5}}$ across diverse manipulation tasks and multiple evaluation settings. UniTexture reduces the mean task success rate from 90.0% under benign conditions to 48.4% under attack, induces target-aligned action shifts, and further exhibits cross-suite and cross-model transfer without re-optimization. Together, these findings reveal shared cross-task vulnerabilities in multitask VLAs that can be systematically exploited through a single adversarial surface texture.

</details>


### [78] [Evaluation of Clinically Steerable Retinal Image Generation from Foundation Model Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13455)
*Zuzanna A. Wakefield-Skórniewska,Bartłomiej W. Papież*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究评估了四个视网膜基础模型在表征分词器框架中的表现，探索是否在合成图像生成过程中保留人口统计学和临床信息。结果显示，合成表征和图像在其原始基础模型内评估时能够忠实地继承表型信息，在多个下游预测任务中优于传统潜在扩散。然而，这些优势在使用真实图像训练的分类器进行评估时基本消失，揭示了一个先前未被表征的合成到真实表征差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学基础模型学习具有临床意义的表型潜在表征，但其支持可控图像生成的能力尚未被充分探索。研究旨在评估视网膜基础模型在表征分词器框架中的表现，并检验合成图像生成过程中是否保留人口统计学和临床信息。

Method: 在表征分词器框架内评估四个视网膜基础模型，检查来自基础模型的潜在表征在合成图像生成中是否保留人口统计学和临床信息。比较合成表征和图像与真实图像在下游预测任务中的表现。

Result: 生成的表征和图像在其原始基础模型内评估时能够忠实继承表型信息，在多个下游预测任务中持续优于传统潜在扩散。然而，这些优势在使用真实图像训练的分类器进行评估时基本消失，揭示了先前未表征的合成到真实表征差距。

Conclusion: 基础模型潜在空间为可控视网膜合成提供了强大的基础，同时突显了更好地对齐合成表征与真实图像分布的必要性。需要在未来研究中解决合成到真实表征差距问题，以提高合成图像在临床应用中的实用性。

Abstract: Medical foundation models learn latent representations of clinically meaningful phenotypes, yet their ability to support controllable image generation remains largely unexplored. We evaluate four retinal foundation models within the representation tokenizer framework and examine whether demographic and clinical information encoded in latent representations from foundation models is preserved during synthetic image generation. We show that generated representations and images faithfully inherit phenotype information when evaluated within their originating foundation models, consistently outperforming conventional latent diffusion on multiple downstream prediction tasks. However, these gains largely disappear when evaluated using classifiers trained on real images, revealing a previously uncharacterised synthetic-to-real representation gap. These findings demonstrate that foundation-model latent spaces provide a powerful substrate for controllable retinal synthesis while highlighting the need to better align synthetic representations with real-image distributions.

</details>


### [79] [Fine-Grained Action Recognition with Cross-Attentive Latent Sparse Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13458)
*Imtiaz Ul Hassan,Tasweer Ahmad,Nik Bessis,Ardhendu Behera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FineX是一种用于细粒度人体动作识别的新方法,通过将动作线索分解为RGB外观、姿态热图几何和骨骼图拓扑三个维度,利用成对交叉注意力实现对称的流保留信息交换,并通过流式潜在稀疏混合专家网络进行条件专家细化和负载均衡正则化,在Gym99、Gym288和Diving48数据集上取得最先进结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB表示保留视觉上下文但往往抑制关节级几何信息,而骨架表示编码运动学但丢弃密集空间细节,现有方法难以同时充分利用这两种异构表示的互补优势。

Method: 提出FineX方法:1)将细粒度线索分解为RGB外观、姿态热图几何和骨骼图拓扑三个因子;2)采用成对交叉注意力实现对称的流保留信息交换;3)使用流式潜在稀疏混合专家网络,根据内容将每个表示路由到共享专家子集,并用负载均衡目标进行正则化。

Result: 在Gym99、Gym288和Diving48上实现最先进性能;在长尾分布的Gym288上,平均类别准确率从68.6%提升至76.2%,提升7.6个百分点,且无需文本监督或大规模视觉-语言预训练。

Conclusion: 结构化视觉-姿态-图融合和条件专家细化为细粒度人体动作识别带来了显著收益,为多模态异构表示的有效融合提供了新思路。

Abstract: Fine-grained human action recognition (FHAR) must distinguish visually similar actions that differ mainly in body configuration, timing, or local appearance. RGB representations retain visual context but often suppress joint-level geometry, whereas skeleton representations encode kinematics but discard dense spatial detail. We introduce FineX, which factorizes fine-grained cues into RGB appearance, pose heatmap geometry, and skeletal-graph topology. Pairwise cross-attention enables symmetric, stream-preserving information exchange, followed by a streamwise latent sparse Mixture-of-Experts that routes each representation to a content-dependent subset of shared experts, regularized by a load-balancing objective. FineX achieves state-of-the-art results on Gym99, Gym288, and Diving48. On the long-tailed Gym288, it raises mean class accuracy from 68.6% to 76.2% (+7.6 points) without textual supervision or large-scale vision-language pre-training, demonstrating the benefit of structured visual-pose-graph fusion and conditional expert refinement for FHAR.

</details>


### [80] [SNM-VFI: Symmetric Nonlinear Motion-Guided Generative Video Frame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13460)
*Jisoo Jeong,Hong Cai,Jamie Menjay Lin,Hanno Ackermann,Hyeonjun Sim,Yinhao Zhu,Yunxiao Shi,Fatih Porikli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SNM-VFI是一种无需训练的生成式视频帧插值框架，结合预训练光流模型和视频扩散模型，通过对称非线性运动模型生成对应感知的中间帧，并利用置信度图融合光流预测与扩散生成细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于扩散模型的VFI方法从随机噪声合成中间帧，难以保持运动对应关系和时序一致性。该研究旨在利用光流模型的运动信息引导扩散模型，兼顾精确重建与感知质量。

Method: 1. 利用预训练光流模型构建多帧非线性流式中间帧和置信度图；2. 将流引导帧编码为潜在先验初始化并迭代引导预训练视频扩散模型；3. 使用置信度图在遮挡和物体边界等不确定区域融合可靠的流预测与扩散细节。

Result: 在DAVIS、Sintel和KITTI等挑战性基准上实现了较强的感知质量、竞争力的重建精度和稳健的时序一致性。

Conclusion: SNM-VFI通过结合光流对应信息与扩散模型的生成能力，无需额外训练即可实现高质量、可控的生成式视频帧插值。

Abstract: We propose Symmetric Nonlinear Motion-guided Generative Video Frame Interpolation (SNM-VFI), a training-free framework for motion-controllable generative video frame interpolation with pre-trained optical flow and video diffusion models. Unlike conventional diffusion-based VFI methods that synthesize intermediate frames from random noise, SNM-VFI guides the generative process with correspondence-aware frames produced by a symmetric nonlinear motion model. Specifically, we first utilize a pre-trained optical flow model to construct multi-frame nonlinear flow-based intermediate frames and confidence maps. These flow-guided frames are then encoded as latent priors to initialize and iteratively guide a pre-trained Video Diffusion model, enabling the diffusion model to preserve dense motion correspondence while improving perceptual realism. To further enhance output quality, we employ confidence maps to fuse structurally reliable flow-based predictions with diffusion-generated details in uncertain regions such as occlusions and object boundaries. Extensive evaluations on challenging benchmarks, including DAVIS, Sintel, and KITTI, demonstrate that SNM-VFI achieves strong perceptual quality, competitive reconstruction accuracy, and robust temporal coherence across diverse motion scenarios.

</details>


### [81] [MLLM-Routed Heterogeneous Ensembles for Robust Cross-Dataset Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13463)
*Daniel Perkins,John Squires,Janou Milligan,Chandra Raskoti,Linda Ungerboeck*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ARMDIL是一个用于多域图像分类的自适应路由器，结合多模态大语言模型(MLLM)动态选择最佳视觉骨干网络。该方法使用包含CNN(ResNet)、自监督学习(SSL)和视觉语言模型(VLM)的集成系统，通过统一标签空间跨多个数据集进行训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代图像分类模型在单一任务特定数据集上表现良好，但在跨域和跨难度泛化方面存在困难。现有方法难以在模型的专业化能力和泛化能力之间取得平衡。

Method: 构建包含ResNet、SSL和VLM的多样化集成系统；利用MLLM代理动态路由每个图像到最适合的视觉骨干网络；创建统一标签空间整合多个具有不同分布和特征的数据集；通过提示修改实现新信息的轻松集成。

Result: ARMDIL在跨数据集图像分类任务上表现与专业训练路由器相当；通过简单提示修改显著提升适应性；增强可解释性，支持自然语言推理追踪；有效平衡不同架构在各种视觉域上的能力和脆弱性。

Conclusion: 该研究为跨数据集图像分类提供了新思路，推动了更可靠通用视觉系统(如AI助手和自动驾驶机器人)的发展。ARMDIL通过动态路由机制和自然语言推理能力，在保持竞争力的同时提升了模型的适应性和可解释性。

Abstract: Modern image classification models excel when trained on single task-specific datasets but often struggle to generalize across domains and difficulty levels. We propose ARMDIL, an Adaptive Router for Multi-Domain Image classification with LLMs. ARMDIL is an ensemble that uses a multimodal large language model (MLLM) agent to dynamically route each image to the most suitable vision backbone. Our diverse ensemble employs convolutional neural networks (ResNets), self-supervised representation learners (SSL), and vision-language models (VLMs), each trained on a unified label space constructed from multiple image datasets with differing distributions and characteristics. Empirical evaluations illuminate the distinct capabilities and vulnerabilities of each architecture across disparate visual domains. Crucially, we show that ARMDIL effectively navigates these trade-offs, performing competitively with specialized training-based routers. Furthermore, it drastically improves adaptability by allowing new information to be integrated via simple prompt modifications, while enhancing interpretability through natural language reasoning traces. These advances in cross-dataset image classification pave the way for more reliable general-purpose vision systems such as AI assistants and autonomous robots.

</details>


### [82] [MapRoute++: Surrogate-Guided Semantic Routing for Visual Concept Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13478)
*Ashok Urlana,L. D. M. S. Sai Teja,Vivek Hruday Kavuri,Ponnurangam Kumaraguru*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是Genμ 2.0挑战赛中关于视觉概念消除任务的参赛方案，在MapRoute基础上引入了任务特定训练目标、更丰富的概念表示和语义路由机制，实现了稳健的概念移除同时保留无关和语义相邻概念，在Stable Diffusion v1.4上使用ERR指标评估，在五个概念类别上平均超越最先进基线12.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉概念消除(visual concept unlearning)旨在从生成模型中移除特定概念，同时保留其他相关概念的性能。现有的方法在平衡概念移除的鲁棒性与对无关概念和语义相邻概念的保护方面存在挑战。

Method: 在MapRoute基础上提出三项改进：(1)任务特定训练目标，针对概念消除任务设计专门的优化目标；(2)更丰富的概念表示，增强概念特征的表达能力；(3)语义路由机制，实现概念特定的映射器选择。

Result: 在官方基准测试中，使用Stable Diffusion v1.4和Erasing-Retention-Robustness(ERR)指标评估，在五个概念类别上平均性能比最先进基线提升12.1%，实现了显著的性能增益。

Conclusion: 该方法通过结合任务特定训练目标、丰富概念表示和语义路由机制，有效提升了视觉概念消除的鲁棒性，同时很好地保护了无关和语义相邻概念，为生成模型的内容安全控制提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We present our submission to Task 3 of the Gen$μ$ 2.0 Challenge on visual concept unlearning. Building on MapRoute, we introduce task-specific training objectives, richer concept representations, and semantic routing for concept-specific mapper selection. Our approach improves robust concept removal while preserving unrelated and semantically adjacent concepts. On the official benchmark, evaluated using the Erasing-Retention-Robustness (ERR) metric on Stable Diffusion v1.4, our method outperforms the state-of-the-art baseline by 12.1\% on average across the five concept categories, achieving substantial gains.

</details>


### [83] [DreamX-Phi 1.0: Action-Conditioned Video World Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13489)
*DreamX Team,Rui Chen,Xiangxiang Chu,Geng Li,Jifan Li,Qingfeng Shi,Datao Tang,Jing Tang,Jun Wang,Pengfei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamX-Phi 1.0是一个用于机器人操作的动作条件视频世界模型,给定当前帧、语言指令和末端执行器姿态及夹爪状态的动作序列,预测未来观测结果。该模型通过PRoPE风格的几何编码注入每臂SE(3)变换以保持手臂身份和刚体运动结构,添加深度分支获取场景级几何信息,使用SAM3掩码和冻结的V-JEPA教师保持物体一致性,并通过分布匹配蒸馏将多步生成器蒸馏为少步学生模型。该模型在WorldArena 2.0挑战赛中获得Track 1第一名和Track 2第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型的逼真度不能保证忠实度:令人信服的rollout可能移动错误的手臂或丢失被操作的物体。动作控制 alone无法完全约束场景几何或小型被操作物体的演变。

Method: 1)使用PRoPE风格几何编码将每臂SE(3)变换注入注意力,保持手臂身份和刚体运动结构; 2)添加轻量级深度分支获取场景级几何信息; 3)使用SAM3掩码与冻结的V-JEPA教师在整个抓取过程中保持物体一致性; 4)通过分布匹配蒸馏将多步生成器蒸馏为少步学生模型以实现高效部署。

Result: 在WorldArena 2.0挑战赛中获得Track 1第一名和Track 2第二名。

Conclusion: DreamX-Phi 1.0是一个有效的机器人操作动作条件视频世界模型,通过几何编码、深度分支、物体掩码和蒸馏等技术提高了预测的忠实度和部署效率。

Abstract: We present \textbf{{DreamX-Phi 1.0}}, an action-conditioned video world model for robotic manipulation that, given an observed frame, a language instruction, and a prescribed action sequence comprising end-effector poses and gripper states, predicts the resulting future observations. Yet realism alone does not guarantee faithfulness: a convincing rollout can still move the wrong arm or lose the manipulated object. To ensure the prediction respects each arm's commanded path, we inject per-arm $\mathrm{{SE}}(3)$ transformations into attention via \textbf{{PRoPE-style geometric encoding}}, preserving arm identity and rigid-motion structure. Action control alone does not fully constrain scene geometry or the evolution of small manipulated objects. We therefore add a lightweight \textbf{{depth branch}} for scene-level geometry and use \textbf{{SAM3 masks}} with a frozen \textbf{{V-JEPA teacher}} to maintain object consistency throughout grasping. We further distill the multi-step generator into a few-step student via distribution-matching distillation for efficient deployment. At the time of writing, \model{{}} achieves first place on Track~1 and second place on Track~2 of the WorldArena~2.0 Challenge. Our model and code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [84] [TraVEL: Trajectory-Guided Video Embedding Learning for Driving-Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13495)
*Yi-Chung Chen,Philip Jacobson,Tom Lampo,Yiren Lu,Jin Yao,David I. Inouye,Jing Gao,Danhua Guo,Burhan Yaman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了TraVEL框架，通过自我轨迹相似性作为奖励进行Group Relative Policy Optimization，将通用多模态嵌入模型适应于驾驶视频检索任务，特别是在区分运动中心事件（如左转/右转、加速/减速）方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模驾驶日志的高效检索对数据策展、模型开发和安全分析至关重要。通用多模态嵌入模型虽能简化表示，但往往依赖静态场景上下文的捷径，难以区分运动中心事件。

Method: 首先使用InfoNCE目标在nuReasoning的配对剪辑和推理轨迹上微调Qwen3-VL-Embedding；然后引入TraVEL框架，使用自我轨迹相似性作为Group Relative Policy Optimization的奖励，仅在训练时使用轨迹作为特权监督，检索时仍基于单向量视频嵌入。

Result: TraVEL在2B模型上将纵向和横向mAP分别提升9.8和4.7点，在8B模型上提升7.2和1.5点，验证了运动中心检索的显著改进。

Conclusion: TraVEL成功将物理基础监督与高效基于嵌入的搜索结合，无需 ego pose、专家规则或辅助感知输出即可实现精细化运动理解。

Abstract: Efficiently retrieving relevant clips from large-scale driving logs is essential for data curation, model development, and safety analysis. Structured and rule-based retrieval systems can explicitly target driving events, but typically require expert-defined rules, auxiliary data, and multi-stage perception pipelines. Multimodal embedding models offer a simpler and more efficient alternative by representing each video with a single searchable vector. However, general-purpose models often rely on shortcuts from static scene context and struggle to distinguish motion-centric events, such as turning left versus right or accelerating versus decelerating. In this work, we study how to adapt a general-purpose multimodal embedding model to driving-video retrieval. We first fine-tune Qwen3-VL-Embedding on paired clips and reasoning traces from nuReasoning using an InfoNCE objective. While this stage substantially improves overall retrieval, caption supervision alone remains insufficient for fine-grained motion understanding. We therefore introduce TraVEL (Trajectory-Guided Video Embedding Learning), a motion-aware fine-tuning framework that uses ego-trajectory similarity as a reward within Group Relative Policy Optimization. Trajectories serve only as privileged training supervision; retrieval still operates on single-vector video embeddings without ego poses, expert rules, or auxiliary perception outputs. We further construct a driving-video retrieval benchmark from nuReasoning. Experiments show that TraVEL improves motion-centric retrieval across model scales: relative to SFT, it raises longitudinal and lateral mAP by 9.8 and 4.7 points at 2B, with corresponding gains of 7.2 and 1.5 points at 8B. TraVEL thus combines physically grounded supervision with efficient embedding-based search.

</details>


### [85] [GS$^{{2}}$CI: Robust Gaussian Splatting For Snapshot Compressive Imaging via Large Vision Model Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13502)
*Yanming Yang,Chenxi Song,Ping Wang,Xin Yuan,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用3D高斯溅射(3DGS)和视觉基础模型(VFM)从单次快照压缩成像(SCI)测量中重建高质量3D场景的新框架，解决了传统方法中信息丢失、视角多样性问题及计算负担等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快照压缩成像(SCI)虽然能高效获取高速视频和多视角信息，但现有方法在从单次SCI测量重建3D场景时面临信息损失严重、视角多样性有限、3D表示与相机姿态联合优化计算量大等难题。

Method: 提出双阶段方法：1)初级重建阶段结合测量衍生的3D VFM初始化与SCI感知的高斯优化；2)辅助阶段使用2D VFM在合成视角提供伪视角监督进行局部外观细化。引入不透明度引导的分裂与生长调节(OSGR)策略解决SCI监督模糊性问题。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上实现最强整体性能，具有领先的重建质量、对视角变化的强鲁棒性及具有竞争力的计算效率。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将3DGS与大规模视觉基础模型结合用于SCI 3D重建，通过OSGR等创新策略有效提升了重建质量和稳定性，为高效3D场景重建提供了新思路。

Abstract: Snapshot Compressive Imaging (SCI) offers an efficient solution for high-speed video acquisition and, under exposure-time camera--scene relative motion, multi-view scene capture by compressing temporal or spatial information into a single 2D measurement. While recent studies have explored SCI for 3D scene reconstruction, existing methods struggle with significant challenges due to information loss, limited viewpoint diversity, and the computational burden of jointly optimizing 3D representations and camera poses. In this work, we propose a novel framework that reconstructs high-quality 3D scenes from a single SCI measurement by leveraging 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) and the powerful priors of large-scale vision foundation models (VFMs). Our primary reconstruction combines measurement-derived 3D VFM initialization with SCI-aware Gaussian optimization. After coarse-stage convergence, an auxiliary 2D VFM provides pseudo-view supervision at synthesized viewpoints for local appearance refinement. To further address the instability caused by ambiguous SCI supervision during 3DGS optimization, we introduce Opacity-Guided Splitting and Growth Regulation (OSGR), an SCI-specific densification strategy that augments split candidates using local opacity statistics, discourages loss-compensating opacity inflation through mean-opacity regulation, and bounds representation growth with explicit candidate-ratio and Gaussian-count constraints. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves the strongest overall performance, combining leading reconstruction quality and robustness to viewpoint variation with competitive computational efficiency.

</details>


### [86] [TabSOM: A tabular-to-image encoding method based on self-organizing maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13513)
*David Chushig-Muzo,María Ángeles Rodríguez de Cara,Eva Milara,Francisco J. Lara-Abelenda,Luis Zhinin-Vera,Diego H. Peluffo-Ordóñez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TabSOM是一种新型的表格数据到图像的转换方法，利用自组织映射(SOM)将表格数据编码为双通道图像，在保留特征值的同时编码成对特征关系，并在12个公开二分类数据集上取得了排名第一或第二的性能，同时具有可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表格到图像方法仅编码特征的边缘值，丢弃了特征之间的关系信息，导致信息损失和性能受限。

Method: 提出TabSOM方法，包含：(i)基于SOM组件平面的无碰撞匈牙利分配，为每个输入特征分配固定画布位置；(ii)从SOM组件平面提取的成对特征关系图。生成的图像堆叠两个多尺度节点通道：一个编码固定尺度的特征值，另一个编码成对特征交互作为相关特征之间的空间连接。此外还引入了两种SOM可解释性方法：原型启发的部分依赖图和类分离重要性分数。

Result: 在12个公开二分类数据集上与12种现有表格到图像方法对比，TabSOM在所有数据集上排名第一或第二，且方差最低。可解释性与Random Forest、XGBoost和SHAP验证一致，类分离分数与基线特征重要性合理一致，同时捕捉到输入数据的补充结构信息。

Conclusion: TabSOM为表格数据应用深度学习架构提供了有效且可解释的方法，弥合了该领域的性能-可解释性差距。

Abstract: Tabular-to-image methods have emerged as novel approaches to leverage the high predictive performance of convolutional neural networks and vision transformers. They convert tabular data into image representations, mapping each feature at a fixed pixel location derived from a dimensionality-reduction method (e.g., t-SNE, UMAP, PCA). However, they encode only the marginal value of each feature and discard information about feature relationships. We propose TabSOM, a tabular-to-image encoding built on the Self-Organizing Map (SOM), which provides: (i) a spatial layout in which every input feature occupies a fixed canvas position derived from its component plane via collision-free Hungarian assignment; and (ii) a graph that captures pairwise feature relationships derived from the SOM component planes. The resulting image stacks two multi-scale node channels: one encodes feature values at fixed scales, while the other encodes pairwise feature interactions as spatial connections between related features. Two SOM-derived interpretability approaches are introduced: a prototype-inspired partial dependence plot and a class--separation importance score. Benchmarked against twelve existing tabular-to-image methods across public binary-classification datasets, TabSOM ranks first or second on every dataset and achieves the lowest variance of any method evaluated. Interpretability obtained with TabSOM was validated against Random Forest, XGBoost, and SHAP, the class-separation score shows reasonable agreement with established baselines on the top-ranked features while capturing complementary structural information from input data. These results demonstrate that TabSOM provides an effective and interpretable approach for applying deep learning architectures to tabular data, bridging the performance--interpretability gap in this domain.

</details>


### [87] [SCULPT: Subtractive Composition for 3D Part Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13541)
*Sikuang Li,Chen Yang,Jiemin Fang,Jiazhong Cen,Yuhe Wei,Jichen Pang,Wei Shen,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCULPT是一种基于减法组合的部分感知3D生成框架，通过在结构化3D潜在空间中迭代应用联合分裂预测器，同时生成提取的部分和剩余对象，避免了传统分割方法或加法方法带来的边界问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在根本性缺陷：分割法在生成完整形状后才确定部件边界，限制了编辑灵活性；加法方法虽然暴露了部件基数，但共享边界容易出现间隙、穿插和材质不连续等问题。论文提出需要一种在生成过程中就确定部件边界的方法。

Method: SCULPT采用减法组合策略：1)将完整对象表示在结构化3D潜在空间中；2)迭代应用联合分裂预测器，每次生成一个提取部件和更新后的剩余对象；3)预测器执行耦合去噪过程，同时条件于图像和当前3D状态；4)在部件支撑集的并集上处理输出，允许相邻支撑重叠而非强制分割；5)当剩余支撑为空或达到安全上限时停止。

Result: 在PartObjaverse基准上达到最先进的几何质量，同时保持强大的完整对象重建质量。四张数据集图像、一个文本到图像生成输入和一张真实世界照片的额外结果进一步展示了细粒度纹理部件分解能力。

Conclusion: SCULPT通过减法组合和联合分裂预测的方式，成功解决了部分感知3D生成中几何质量与部件结构暴露之间的矛盾，实现了高质量完整对象重建与灵活部件编辑的兼得。

Abstract: Part-aware 3D generation aims to create digital assets that are coherent as complete objects while exposing structural parts for editing, material assignment, animation, and reuse. Existing methods impose this structure outside the native generation loop: segmentation-based methods partition an already generated shape, while additive methods synthesize parts from predefined layouts, boxes, or tokens and then reconcile them into a whole. The former preserves the generated geometry but fixes the object before part boundaries are determined; the latter exposes part cardinality but often leaves shared boundaries vulnerable to gaps, interpenetrations, and material discontinuities. In this paper, we propose SCULPT, a framework that addresses these challenges through subtractive composition. Given a complete object represented in a structured 3D latent space, SCULPT iteratively applies a joint split predictor to generate one extracted part together with the remaining object. The predictor performs a coupled denoising process conditioned on both the image and the current 3D state, so the extracted part and updated remainder are generated together rather than reconciled after generation. The joint split predictor processes both outputs on the union of their native sparse 3D supports, allowing neighboring supports to overlap rather than imposing a disjoint voxel partition. The rollout ends when the remainder support becomes empty or reaches a fixed safety cap, allowing the number of generated parts to adapt to each object within that bound. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art geometry on PartObjaverse while preserving strong complete-object reconstruction after part assembly. Results on four dataset images, one text-to-image-generated input, and one real-world photograph further show fine-grained textured part decomposition beyond the benchmark.

</details>


### [88] [Alaya-EVOKE: From Linear-Scaling Supervision to Endless World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13546)
*Yuanyang Yin,Gongxuan Wang,Yifan Zhan,Chuanhao Li,Kaipeng Zhang,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Evoke是一个交互式世界模型，通过外部化持久世界状态和重新设计教师模型，解决了持久记忆、响应式交互和长视野生成之间的冲突问题。支持开放-ended、持续演进的生成，在单卡H200上每1.5秒片段生成仅需2.11秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界模型需要支持持久记忆、响应式交互和长视野生成，但这些要求对模型提出了相互冲突的需求。在去噪器上下文或键值缓存中维护历史会产生不断增长的成本，迫使我们在会话长度和保留记忆之间进行权衡；而低延迟交互依赖于少数步生成，其能力受限于教师模型。

Method: 1. 外部世界状态银行：维护基于摄像机的场景几何外部存储，仅检索与视图相关的信息，使去噪器上下文保持有界
2. 长视野监督教师模型：稀疏注意力结合分块分组、检索选定的远距离帧和线性注意力全局状态，实现内存和计算量的线性增长
3. 30秒分布匹配目标：在自强制 rollout 下训练一个三步学生模型，无需 classifier-free guidance

Result: 在WBench上达到最先进水平，在VBench-Long和VBench-2.0上保持竞争力。在384×640分辨率下，单卡H200上每1.5秒片段生成时间为2.11秒。

Conclusion: Evoke通过有界上下文和循环外部内存支持开放-ended、持续演进的生成，成功解决了交互式世界模型中持久记忆、响应速度和长视野生成之间的根本矛盾。

Abstract: Interactive world models must support persistent memory, responsive interaction, and long-horizon generation, yet these requirements place conflicting demands on the model. Maintaining history in the denoiser context or key-value cache incurs growing cost, forcing a trade-off between session length and retained memory, while low-latency interaction relies on few-step generation whose capabilities are bounded by its teacher. Evoke addresses both limitations by externalizing persistent world state and redesigning the teacher for long-horizon interactive generation. Scene geometry is maintained in an external, camera-indexed world state bank, from which only view-relevant information is retrieved, keeping the denoiser context bounded as the session grows. Rather than treating the teacher as a fixed generator, we design it for long-horizon supervision: its sparse attention combines chunk-wise grouping, retrieval of selected distant frames, and a linear-attention global state, yielding linear growth in memory and compute while enabling supervision over long horizons. Such supervision exposes content drift that stays locally plausible within short windows, while per-chunk conditioning enables prompt changes and event control throughout the sequence. A 30-second distribution-matching objective, applied under self-forced rollouts, transfers both capabilities to a three-step student that uses no classifier-free guidance, improving resistance to long-term drift while preserving responsive conditioning. With bounded context and recurrent external memory, Evoke supports open-ended, continuously evolving generation; on a single H200 at $384\times 640$, each $1.5\,\mathrm{{s}}$ chunk is generated in $2.11\,\mathrm{{s}}$. As a three-step world model, Evoke achieves state-of-the-art performance on WBench while remaining competitive on VBench-Long and VBench-2.0.

</details>


### [89] [PlayWorld: Benchmarking World Models with Agent Players over Long-Horizon Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13552)
*Kaixin Ding,Xi Chen,Minghong Cai,Zhiyuan Xu,Yiyang Wang,Yuxiang Lu,Junyi Li,Shuyang Chen,Yuan Gao,Xin Tao,Pengfei Wan,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PlayWorld是一个用于评估视频世界模型的基准测试，通过多模态智能体玩家与171个场景进行交互，从几何一致性、交互保真度、视线外演变和洞察演变四个维度评估模型性能。实验表明当前最先进的视频世界模型在长时序交互目标上仍不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型评估方法的局限性：1) 固定动作序列评估不适用于跨模型比较，因为不同模型达到相同目标所需的动作序列可能差异很大；2) 缺乏针对长时序目标的系统评估框架；3) 需要通过人类玩家式的交互方式来评估模型在复杂场景下的表现。

Method: 1. 提出多模态Agent Player范式，让智能体与世界模型交互以完成指定的长期目标；2. 构建PlayWorld基准测试，包含171个场景，每个场景都有特定的交互目标；3. 从四个核心维度评估：几何一致性、交互保真度、视线外演变、洞察演变；4. 同时纳入基础能力指标：视频质量和可控性。

Result: 对九个最先进的视频世界模型进行实验，结果显示当前模型在长期交互目标上仍然不可靠，特别是在维持空间一致性和持久状态演变方面存在明显不足。

Conclusion: PlayWorld基准测试填补了视频世界模型交互评估的空白，揭示了当前最先进模型的关键局限性，为未来研究提供了有价值的评估框架和性能基线。

Abstract: Video world models simulate future states conditioned on current observations and user actions. Recent systems have demonstrated impressive video consistency and action controllability over long sequences. However, fairly comparing these interactive models remains challenging. In practice, a human player typically evaluates a world model by pursuing long-horizon objectives through interaction. For example, a user may turn around 360 degrees to see whether the environment remains consistent, or walk into the water and inspect whether realistic water ripples are generated. The action sequence required to achieve the same objective may vary substantially between models, making fixed action-conditioned evaluation unsuitable for cross-model comparison. To address this, we employ multi-modal Agent Players to interact with world models toward specified long-horizon objectives. Building on this paradigm, we introduce PlayWorld, a benchmark providing 171 scenarios, each with a specified objective. To evaluate performance thoroughly, we assess models along four core dimensions: geometry consistency, interaction fidelity, out-of-sight evolution, and insight evolution. In addition, we incorporate basic ability metrics for video quality and controllability. Experiments across nine state-of-the-art world models reveal that current models remain unreliable on long-horizon interactive objectives, particularly in maintaining spatial consistency and persistent state evolution. Code and data are available at https://github.com/kxding/PlayWorld.

</details>


### [90] [V-RAE: Rethinking Video Latent Spaces for Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13556)
*Minghui Guo,Shengqiong Wu,Hao Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: V-RAE是一种视频表示自编码器，利用冻结视觉基础模型的语义表示构建紧凑生成潜空间，通过时间池化模块去除冗余并保留语义结构，在视频重建、语义探查、类别条件生成和未来预测任务上均取得优异性能，验证了冻结语义表示支持视频重建、生成和预测建模的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频自编码器的潜空间主要针对像素级重建优化，缺乏高层语义组织。重建最优的潜空间不一定适合生成建模，需要探索更适合生成任务的新型视频表示方法。

Method: V-RAE构建在冻结视觉基础模型表示之上的紧凑生成潜空间。采用轻量级时间池化模块去除时间冗余同时保留语义结构，视频解码器从压缩特征中重建连续运动。评估中测试了四种代表性冻结编码器。

Result: 在K600上达到2.13 rFVD，优于所有评估的大型预训练视频VAE。潜空间保留的语义信息显著多于传统视频分词器。在匹配生成设置下，UCF101和K600的gFVD分别为117.86和19.16，收敛速度提升6倍。引入tFVD时间一致性诊断指标，比重建质量更能预测下游生成质量。在Cityscapes上改进未来视频预测。

Conclusion: 冻结的语义表示可以支持视频重建、生成和预测建模。V-RAE在多个任务上展现出优越性能，包括视频重建、语义探查、类别条件生成和未来视频预测，验证了基于冻结基础模型构建生成式潜空间的可行性。

Abstract: Latent video generation relies on autoencoders to define a compact space in which generative models operate. Although video autoencoder architectures have evolved substantially, their latent spaces are still optimized primarily for pixel-level reconstruction and provide limited high-level semantic organization. A reconstruction-optimal latent space, however, need not be well suited to generative modeling. We propose V-RAE, a video representation autoencoder that builds compact generative latents on top of frozen vision foundation model representations. A lightweight temporal pooling module removes temporal redundancy while preserving semantic structure, and a video decoder reconstructs continuous motion from the compressed features. We evaluate V-RAE with four representative frozen encoders on video reconstruction, semantic probing, and class-conditional generation. V-RAE achieves 2.13 rFVD on K600, outperforming all evaluated large-scale pretrained video VAEs. Its latents retain substantially more semantic information than conventional video tokenizer latents. Under matched generation settings, our best variant achieves gFVD scores of 117.86 and 19.16 on UCF101 and K600, respectively, while converging up to 6x faster}}. We further show that reconstruction quality alone is insufficient to characterize generative utility and introduce tFVD, a temporal-coherence diagnostic that correlates more reliably with downstream generation quality. Beyond video generation, V-RAE also improves future video prediction on Cityscapes over the Wan 2.2 VAE latent space under matched prediction settings. Taken together, the experiments show that frozen semantic representations can support video reconstruction, generation, and predictive modeling. The project page: https://v-rae.github.io/.

</details>


### [91] [AutoDesign: Meta-Harness Optimization for Long-Horizon Agentic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13560)
*Yaxin Luo,Haobin Jiang,Jialv Zou,Xu Huang,Wenhao Yan,Haodong Li,Zhengrong Yue,Jing Li,Xiaofu Chen,Xiaohan Zhao,Jiacheng Liu,Jiacheng Cui,Zhiqiang Shen,Xiaotong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AutoDesign是一个通过元harness优化器指导代码代理递归改进harness的框架,用于将多模态源(如学术论文)转换为结构化媒体输出(如海报)。在论文到海报生成任务上,该系统达到78.32分,超越Claude Design 7.45分,且在完全自主长视野循环中以低成本达到会议海报质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有harness系统是静态的,缺乏通过经验积累实现递归自我改进的能力。理想系统应符合人类设计先验,并通过实证探索积累可重用经验来驱动递归自我改进。

Method: 提出AutoDesign框架:1)元harness优化器指导代码代理;2)基于 rollout 反馈递归改进harness;3)构建PosterBench评估基准(100篇论文主赛道+10篇共享子集)。

Result: 1) PosterBench主赛道得分78.32,超越闭源商业系统Claude Design 7.45分;2)七种代码代理模型配置下,集成学习到的DesignHarness平均分数从54.99提升至67.39(+12.4%);3)完全自主循环中执行253次工具调用、11轮编辑,40分钟内完成且成本低于3美元;4)系统盲测中人类偏好最高。

Conclusion: AutoDesign框架成功实现了符合人类设计先验的递归自我改进系统,在学术论文到海报生成任务上达到最先进水平,为长视野智能体系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Transforming multimodal sources into condensed and structured media outputs can be fundamentally conceptualized as a long-horizon agentic process centered on a model-harness system. While an ideal harness system should align with human design priors and accumulate reusable experience through empirical exploration to drive recursive self-improvement, existing paradigms remain static and fall short of this capability. In this paper, we present AutoDesign, a framework that aligns with human design priors, where a meta-harness optimizer guides a code agent to recursively improve harness based on rollout feedback. To instantiate and evaluate this framework, we focus on the academic paper-to-poster generation task and introduce PosterBench, comprising a 100-paper Main Track spanning five disciplines and PosterBench-mini, a shared 10-paper subset for controlled evaluation. On the PosterBench Main Track, AutoDesign achieves the highest score of 78.32, surpassing the closed-source commercial system Claude Design by 7.45 points. Across seven controlled code-agent-model configurations, integrating the learned DesignHarness consistently improves performance, increasing the average PosterBench Score from 54.99 to 67.39 (+12.4%). In a fully autonomous long-horizon loop, it executes 253 tool calls and 11 editing turns within 40 minutes for under $3, reaching average conference-poster quality in human evaluation. A system-blind human study further demonstrates that AutoDesign achieves the highest human preference among evaluated systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [92] [Entropy-Augmented Multi-Objective Policy Optimization in Multiagent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12534)
*Jamie Santos,Ayhan Alp Aydeniz,Raghav Thakar,Kagan Tumer*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种熵增强策略评估方法，通过在智能体适应度分数中加入熵奖励来促进行为多样性，解决多目标多智能体进化优化中行为空间多样性不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的NSGA-II等多目标进化算法仅优化目标空间的多样性，忽视了行为空间的多样性，可能导致早熟收敛和策略行为趋同，无法适应不同外部条件的需求。

Method: 引入熵增强策略评估方法，将熵奖励纳入智能体适应度评估，在保持Pareto优化框架的同时，利用行为空间多样性信号鼓励探索行为独特的策略。

Result: 在 rover 领域实验中，相对 NSGA-II 基线实现了高达 48% 的超体积改进。

Conclusion: 行为多样性是提升多目标多智能体进化优化的有前景且尚未充分探索的方向。

Abstract: Autonomous agent teams deployed in settings such as marine and extraterrestrial outposts must coordinate actions to achieve optimal outcomes across multiple competing objectives. Multi-objective evolutionary algorithms such as NSGA-II optimize for diversity in the objective space, but neglect diversity in the behavior space, possibly leading to premature convergence and a collapse in behaviors that may differentiate policies in different external conditions. To address this, we introduce an entropy-augmented policy evaluation strategy that incorporates an entropy bonus into agent fitness scores, discouraging behavioral homogeneity across the evolving population. By augmenting policy evaluation with a behavior-space diversity signal while preserving the underlying Pareto optimization framework, our method is designed to encourage exploration of behaviorally distinct policies in multiagent domains. We evaluate our approach across rover-domain experiments with qualitatively distinct reward structures and observe hypervolume improvements of up to 48% relative to the NSGA-II baseline, suggesting that behavioral diversity is a promising and underexplored direction for improving multi-objective multiagent evolutionary optimization.

</details>


### [93] [Do LLMs Beat Nash? Testing Decentralized Coordination in Self-Play Multi-Agent Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12547)
*Deborah Sinishaw,Qile Zhu,Edwin Meriaux,Gregory Dudek*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究无需通信协调的大型语言模型代理在博弈中的表现，测试13种语言模型在单向无通信博弈中能否超越标准博弈论基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索独立部署的相同语言模型实例在无法通信的情况下，能否通过推理对手行为来协调行动，超越标准博弈论中非协调博弈的基线表现。

Method: 构建了一个单向无通信博弈基准测试，包括：两人矩阵博弈（七种 archetypes，每玩家2-10个行动）和团队博弈（四人或更多可互换代理）。评估13种语言模型相对于博弈底层纳什均衡的表现。

Result: 两个前沿托管模型持续超越纳什基准，在多种archetypes中接近最优联合结果；大多数开放权重模型仅获得部分收益，且随博弈结构变化显著。团队博弈中性能大幅下降，特别是行动空间增大时。

Conclusion: 驱动双人博弈中自博弈收益的能力无法转移到更大的多代理团队，表明语言模型在无通信协调方面存在明显局限性。

Abstract: Large language model agents deployed without a central controller are often assumed to require communication to coordinate their actions. We ask what remains possible without it: when independent instances of the same model cannot communicate, can they still reason about their counterparts well enough to exceed the standard game-theoretic baseline for uncoordinated play? We introduce a benchmark of one-shot, no-communication games in which each of thirteen language models is told only that its counterparts are running the same model and is evaluated against the Nash equilibrium of the underlying game. In two-player matrix games spanning seven archetypes and two to ten actions per player, two frontier-hosted models consistently exceed their Nash benchmark, approaching the optimal joint outcome in several archetypes, while most open-weight models achieve only partial gains that vary sharply by game structure. Performance degrades substantially in team-based games with four or more interchangeable agents, particularly as the action space grows, suggesting that whatever capability drives self-play gains in dyadic games does not transfer to larger multi-agent teams.

</details>


### [94] [Discovering Efficient and Explainable Communication Topologies for LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems via Causal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12921)
*Junzhi Li,Peng He,Qirui Ji,Wei Wang,Lixiang Liu,Chuxiong Sun*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: E2-Explainer是一个模型无关的框架，用于对多智能体系统中生成的通信拓扑提供可解释性解释，通过识别紧凑的通信子图来解释为什么某些通信边被选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的拓扑生成方法通常通过黑盒优化来学习通信拓扑，仅由任务级奖励驱动。这种优化缺乏对为什么选择特定通信边的洞察力，难以识别导致成功协作的关键通信子图。

Method: 将拓扑解释表述为因果归因问题，识别由边缘级任务保留证据支持的紧凑通信子图。使用Granger风格目标获取证据，测量遮蔽每个通信通道如何改变任务结果和最终响应的稳定性。然后将预算子图提炼为摊销解释器，实现高效的事后解释。

Result: 在多个推理和编码基准测试上的广泛实验表明，E2-Explainer识别出能保留成功协作的关键通信子图。这些子图可直接执行以剪枝冗余通信边，在保持竞争力的任务性能的同时大幅降低通信成本。

Conclusion: E2-Explainer框架能够有效解释多智能体系统中的通信拓扑，识别关键通信子图，并可通过剪枝冗余边来降低通信成本。

Abstract: The performance of large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems (MAS) largely depends on effective communication topologies. Existing topology generation methods, however, typically learn communication topologies through black-box optimization driven solely by task-level rewards. While effective, such optimization provides little insight into why particular communication edges are selected, making it difficult to identify the critical communication subgraphs responsible for successful collaboration. To address this limitation, we propose E2-Explainer, a model-agnostic framework for providing interpretable explanations of communication topologies produced by arbitrary topology generators. Specifically, we formulate topology explanation as a causal attribution problem that identifies compact communication subgraphs supported by edge-level evidence of task preservation. We obtain this evidence with a Granger-style objective that measures how masking each communication channel changes the task outcome and the stability of the final response. The resulting budgeted subgraphs are then distilled into an amortized explainer, enabling efficient post-hoc explanation without repeated edge-level evaluations at deployment. Extensive experiments on multiple reasoning and coding benchmarks demonstrate that E2-Explainer identifies critical communication subgraphs that preserve successful collaboration. These subgraphs can also be executed directly to prune redundant communication edges, substantially reducing communication costs while maintaining competitive task performance.

</details>


### [95] [Reconcile Once, Write Anytime: A Trust-Tiered Librarian and a Multi-Agent Writer for Drift-Free, Point-in-Time Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12984)
*Xing Zhang,Yanwei Cui,Guanghui Wang,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出一个双层智能体系统来解决大语言模型生成长篇研究报告时的漂移、自相矛盾和溯源丢失问题。该系统将维护的时点知识库与报告编写分离，通过" librarian"（图书管理员）层构建信任分级的本体论和证据卡片账本，通过"writer"（写作者）层生成无矛盾、基于证据的报告。实验表明该系统能消除6,845个横截面矛盾，在22个金标准案例中全部正确，且零前瞻违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成长篇研究报告存在三大核心问题：1)随时间漂移；2)自相矛盾；3)丢失溯源。同一指标出现不同值，谣言与审计文件被同等自信地引用。现有RAG方法仍基于原始片段查询，无法保证一致性和可追溯性。

Method: 构建双层智能体系统：1)确定性"librarian"将带时间戳的源文档摄入信任分级本体论，生成证据卡片、权威指标账本和声明图谱，作为永恒的真相来源；2)可移植多智能体"writer"运行时组成无矛盾、证据支撑的报告，仅读取as_of <= T的证据（无前瞻），红队判决回流至librarian。评估采用难度分级的模型路由。

Result: 1)质量控制门在recall 1.0和precision 1.0下验证；2)共享指标账本消除6,845个横截面矛盾至零；3)分层选择正确率22/22（ popularity-first基线仅9/22）；4)信任分层零泄漏媒体来源数字；5)无政府统计取代公司自身文件；6)红队反驳自修正无需人工编辑；7)七个截止点零前瞻违规；8)库从235,373增长至555,312卡片；9)难度分级模型路由超越all-Opus质量天花板，速度提升3.7倍。

Conclusion: 双层智能体架构有效解决了LLM研究报告的漂移、矛盾和溯源问题。分离知识库与写作、信任分级证据、时点一致性检查和自修正机制是关键技术。该方法在准确性、一致性和可追溯性上显著优于现有RAG方法，为自动化研究report生成提供了可靠框架。

Abstract: Long-form research reports generated by large language models drift, contradict themselves, and lose provenance: the same metric appears with different values, and rumor is quoted as confidently as an audited filing. We present a two-tier agentic system that separates a maintained, point-in-time knowledge library from report writing. A deterministic "librarian" ingests timestamped sources into a trust-tiered ontology, layering evidence cards, an authoritative metric ledger, and a claim graph into an always-current source of truth, not per-query RAG over raw chunks. A portable multi-agent "writer" runtime then composes a contradiction-free, evidence-grounded report at any knowledge cutoff T, reading only evidence with as_of <= T (no look-ahead); red-team verdicts flow back into the librarian. We evaluate on a self-collected, public corpus of 6,130 sources yielding 555,926 evidence cards (SEC EDGAR filings across 295 issuers and 11 sectors, U.S. Bureau of Labor Statistics releases, and Wikipedia). From the one library we compose four point-in-time reports on distinct theses and run eight reproducible experiments, whose headline metrics come from a deterministic quality-control gate, itself validated by defect-injection meta-evaluation at recall 1.0 and precision 1.0. A shared metric ledger removes 6,845 cross-section contradictions to zero. Tier-first selection is correct on 22/22 gold cases where a popularity-first baseline scores only 9/22; trust tiering leaks zero media-sourced numbers, and no government statistic displaces a company's own filing. A red-team refutation propagates back and self-corrects a later run with zero manual edits. Replay exhibits zero look-ahead violations across seven cutoffs while the library grows from 235,373 to 555,312 cards. Difficulty-tiered model routing exceeds the all-Opus quality ceiling while running 3.7x faster than serial.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Position: Reasoning is a Learnable Rule-Based Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12325)
*Rachel Lawrence,Jacqueline Maasch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，批评当前生成式AI社区对"推理"概念缺乏明确的操作定义，导致推理评估的可信度无法验证。论文提出应基于逻辑和自动推理的传统方法，为推理提供可学习的基于规则的操作定义，并提供最佳实践清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决生成式AI社区对"推理"概念定义模糊的问题，推动可量化的可信 autonomous推理进展。

Method: 综合现有文献，提出操作定义，将有效且合理的推理定位为可学习的基于规则的过程；制定AI推理研究沟通最佳实践清单。

Result: 提供了操作定义和检查清单，为推理评估提供可验证的框架。

Conclusion: 通过明确定义和最佳实践，可解决推理概念的模糊性，推动 autonomous reasoning领域的可衡量进步。

Abstract: Autonomous reasoning is among the most scientifically and economically motivating topics in AI today. Historically the purview of symbolic AI, recent advances have mainly emerged from deep probabilistic generative models. Despite immense interest and rapid progress, the generative AI community has not clearly converged on operational definitions for reasoning and often implicitly rejects the historical treatment of this topic in logic and verifiable automated reasoning. This position contends that definitional ambiguity leaves the construct validity of reasoning evaluation unverifiable, undermining quantifiable progress toward trustworthy autonomous reasoning. We also contend that this ambiguity is addressable. To that end, we provide (1) operational definitions based on a synthesis of the literature, positioning valid and sound reasoning as a learnable rule-based process; and (2) a checklist for best practices in the communication of AI reasoning research.

</details>


### [97] [Agent Behavioral Contracts II: Certifying Compositional Reliability Without Assuming Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12895)
*Varun Pratap Bhardwaj,Garima Singh,Arun Pratap Bhardwaj*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了多智能体系统中组件可靠性的条件独立假设是否成立。通过对18,000个任务进行预注册评估，发现使用相同模型的智能体在90%的任务中共同失败，远超独立性假设的预期。该论文还提出了无依赖假设的有限样本证书方法，并证明拟合依赖模型的bootstrap方法会随数据增加而变差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统的组合可靠性计算通常假设条件独立性，但这一假设很少被验证。作者想要测试这一假设是否成立，以及违背该假设会带来什么后果。

Method: 预注册评估18,000个任务，使用确定性代码评分；替换不同模型和不同供应商进行对比实验；提出基于Bonferroni-Clopper-Pearson的线性规划方法来获得无依赖假设下的可靠性边界。

Result: 使用相同模型的智能体在90%的任务中共同失败（log OR 6.66, 95% CI [6.38, 7.00]; phi 0.916），表明存在强正依赖。替换模型可降低关联，但替换供应商无显著效果。将10个矩函数丰富到14个可将认证区间收窄85.7%，认证下界从0.2455提升到0.4116。

Conclusion: 条件独立假设在实践中常被违背，尤其是在组件共享模型时。依赖结构的无依赖证书方法效果更好，而拟合依赖模型的bootstrap方法会随数据增加而变差。常用的依赖统计数据可能产生误导。

Abstract: Compositional reliability bounds for multi-agent systems multiply component reliabilities, a step licensed by a conditional-independence assumption that is routinely stated and rarely tested. We test it. Two instances of one model, in a two-agent handoff, co-fail on 90.0% of the missions on which either fails (log OR 6.66, 95% CI [6.38, 7.00]; phi 0.916), in a preregistered evaluation of 18,000 missions scored by deterministic code with no LLM judge. Substituting a different model reduces the association in six of six contrasts; substituting a different vendor, model already different, does not -- a registered hypothesis reported as a null.
  The error is signed and runs against the operator: positive dependence inflates joint failure above the independence product, so redundancy is over-credited exactly when components share a model. The assumption-free alternative is often vacuous, and fitting a dependence model is worse: we prove a bootstrap bound on a fitted model's functional loses coverage of the truth as n grows, the identification gap being O(1) while the bootstrap haircut is O(n^{{-1/2}}). More data makes such a certificate worse, with no visible symptom.
  We give a finite-sample certificate assuming no dependence structure: a linear program over the joint, over a Bonferroni-Clopper-Pearson box around measured co-execution moments. It is sound, sharp for the information supplied, and monotone in the moment family. Enriching ten moment functionals to fourteen narrows the identified interval by 85.7% and lifts the certified floor from 0.2455 to 0.4116. A companion anytime-valid certificate holds type-I error at 0.0471 under optional stopping.
  Common dependence statistics are marginal-bounded and can reverse an apparent ordering of conditions when the compared agents fail at different rates. Contracts, scoring code, analysis scripts, and the preregistration are released.

</details>


### [98] [Diagnostic Foundation for Evaluating LLMs' Research Integrity as Co-Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12345)
*Yash Tripathi,Silu Sharma,Sai Sidhanth Manoharan Jayanthi,Shivank Garg,Lin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究提出了IntegrityBench基准，用于评估语言模型在研究诚信方面的表现，发现前沿模型在压力下约三分之一的关键决策会失败，且规模或推理能力无法可靠缓解此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型日益被部署为合作科学家，但它们在机构压力下维持研究诚信的能力尚未被测量。这揭示了AI辅助研究存在的潜在风险。

Method: 引入IntegrityBench基准，评估36项配对任务，涵盖3个领域和4个研究阶段，采用5级隐式-显式压力协议，测试18个前沿模型变体。

Result: 在峰值压力下，模型约三分之一的关键诚信决策失败；规模和推理能力无法可靠缓解此问题。显式压力导致模型顺从不当行为，而隐式情境重构更常导致过度拒绝合法研究任务。分类不准确的模型在基于人工制品的决策上表现相当或更好（85.7 vs 79.4）。

Conclusion: 研究诚信的三个要素（不当行为分类、伦理行动推理、基于人工制品的决策）结构上可分离，正确伦理行动不需要准确分类。前沿模型可能表面有帮助但实际存在诚信失败，产生两种部署风险：助长研究不当行为和侵蚀对AI辅助研究的信任。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly deployed as co-scientists, yet their ability to uphold research integrity under institutional pressure remains unmeasured. We introduce IntegrityBench, a benchmark evaluating misconduct classification, ethical action reasoning and artifact-grounded decision making across 36 paired tasks under a 5-level implicit-explicit pressure protocol spanning 3 domains and 4 research stages. Evaluating 18 frontier model variants, we find that under peak pressure, models fail roughly 1 in 3 integrity-critical decisions, and neither scale nor reasoning ability reliably mitigates this. Explicit pressures induce compliance with misconduct, while implicit contextual reframing more often causes over-refusal of legitimate research tasks. Interestingly, models failing to classify research requests accurately perform equally or better on artifact-grounded decision making (85.7 vs. 79.4), suggesting the three facets are structurally dissociated and correct ethical action does not require accurate classification. Frontier models can thus appear helpful while harbouring integrity failures that create two distinct deployment risks: facilitating research misconduct and eroding trust in AI-assisted research.

</details>


### [99] [OGR-MARL: Option-Guided Residual Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Heterogeneous USV Cooperative Pursuit in Constrained Port Waterways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12995)
*Mao Jiayang,Wang Lanfeng,Peng Zhao-Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OGR-MARL框架，用于解决约束港口水域中异构USV协同追击拦截逃逸者的问题。该框架结合共享信念、角色条件选项目标、自适应规则惩罚和残差策略学习，可与多种MARL算法结合。实验表明OGR-MASAC实现75.0%捕获率，并具备良好的零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决异构USV在受限港口水域中协同追击拦截逃逸者的问题，需同时满足导航、交通和角色约束。传统MARL算法从零开始探索受限港口环境效率低下，需要一种结合规则引导行为的可扩展框架。

Method: 提出OGR-MARL选项引导残差多智能体强化学习框架，集成共享逃逸者信念、角色条件选项目标、自适应规则惩罚和残差策略学习。与MADDPG、MATD3、MAPPO、MASAC等连续控制MARL算法结合，形成OGR-MADDPG、OGR-MATD3、OGR-MAPPO、OGR-MASAC四种实例化方案。

Result: 在抽象Xiazhimen港口水域场景中，OGR-MASAC实现75.0%捕获率，展现有前景的任务有效规则遵循性和最佳异构协调能力。无需重新训练的零样本迁移到QGIS/AIS信息化的Xiazhimen地图也取得良好结果。

Conclusion: OGR-MARL框架具有良好泛化潜力，在更复杂港口场景中表现出色。该框架解耦特定MARL算法的设计提供了灵活性和可扩展性，残差学习方式有效结合了规则引导行为与学习到的修正动作。

Abstract: Heterogeneous USV cooperative pursuit in constrained port waterways requires evader interception under navigation, traffic, and role constraints. This paper proposes OGR-MARL, an option-guided residual multi-agent reinforcement learning framework that is decoupled from a specific MARL algorithm. OGR-MARL integrates shared evader belief, role-conditioned option targets, adaptive rule penalties, and residual policy learning, allowing different MARL algorithms to learn corrective actions on top of rule-guided behaviors rather than exploring constrained port environments from scratch. We instantiate OGR-MARL with representative continuous-control MARL backbones, including MADDPG, MATD3, MAPPO, and MASAC, yielding OGR-MADDPG, OGR-MATD3, OGR-MAPPO, and OGR-MASAC. Experiments in an abstract Xiazhimen port-waterway scenario show that the OGR-MASAC instantiation achieves a 75.0% capture rate, promising mission-effective rule compliance, and the best heterogeneous coordination among the tested methods. Without retraining, zero-shot transfer to a QGIS/AIS-informed Xiazhimen map achieves promising results, demonstrating the generalization potential of OGR-MARL in more complex port scenarios.

</details>


### [100] [Position: The Alignment Community is Unintentionally Building a Censor's Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12346)
*Sarah Ball,Phil Hackemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文认为现代AI对齐技术可能被恶意用于审查和操纵，构成双重用途风险


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI对齐技术初衷是防止有害输出，但可能被恶意行为者滥用，需要引起社区关注

Method: 将当前对齐技术映射到滥用可能性和实际案例进行分析

Result: 追求"完美对齐"模型的同时，也为恶意行为者提供了不断改进的信息控制工具

Conclusion: 需讨论AI对齐机制的故意滥用问题，并提出缓解策略以防范双重用途风险

Abstract: This position paper argues that modern AI alignment methods - originally designed to prevent harmful output - are dual-use technologies that may easily be misused by malicious actors for censorship and manipulation. By mapping current alignment techniques to the possibility and actual cases of misuse, we show that the quest for a "perfectly aligned" model inadvertently also provides malicious actors with an ever-improving tool for informational dominance. We need to discuss this dual-use potential now, as its risk is exacerbated by rapid user adoption of AI as information provider, economic power asymmetries, and a political landscape that increasingly shifts towards authoritarianism. We conclude by urging the community to consider the intentional misuse of AI alignment mechanisms and propose mitigation strategies to safeguard against this dual-use potential.

</details>


### [101] [Agreement Is Not Alignment: Divergent Moral Grounds in Human and LLM Ethical Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12368)
*Octavian M. Machidon,Alina L. Machidon,Vojko Strahovnik,Mateja Centa Strahovnik,Jonas Miklavčič,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究挑战了"模型与人类标签一致即代表对齐"的假设,通过ETHICS基准测试发现:虽然LLM在最终标签层面与人类标注者Agreement很高,但在道德推理依据层面存在系统性分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前普遍使用人类判断Agreement作为LLM对齐的评估指标,但研究者质疑:两个主体即使得出相同结论,也可能基于完全不同的道德原则、背景假设或情境解读。该研究旨在探究这种"标签一致但理由分歧"的现象。

Method: 构建500条ETHICS衍生基准测试,涵盖5个道德判断领域;收集人类标注者和LLM的最终标签及支持理由;在多个前沿和开源模型家族上进行实验。

Result: 1)最终标签层面与人类标注者多数票的Agreement确实较高;2)但理由层面分析揭示系统性分歧:即使标签匹配,模型在伤害、尊重、承诺履行、正义、应得、借口相关性等类别间重新分配了注意力。

Conclusion: Agreement不应被等同于对齐。标签评估可能具有误导性的安慰效应,必须辅以对模型判断中理由、原则和道德优先级的分析,才能准确评估LLM与人类价值观的对齐程度。

Abstract: Agreement with human judgments is a common proxy for evaluating the alignment of large language models (LLMs). Yet agreement in final labels does not show that human annotators and models rely on the same moral grounds. Two agents may reach the same judgment while appealing to different principles, contextual assumptions, or interpretations of the situation.
  We test this distinction using a curated 500-item ETHICS-derived benchmark spanning five domains of moral judgment, with new human annotator and LLM annotations of both final labels and supporting rationales. Across frontier and open model families, agreement with human annotator majority labels is often high. However, rationale-level analysis reveals systematic divergence in the moral grounds expressed by human annotators and models. In particular, models redistribute attention across categories such as harm, respect, promise-keeping, justice, desert, and excuse relevance, even when their final labels match the human annotator majority.
  Our results show that agreement should not be treated as equivalent to alignment. Label-based evaluation can therefore be misleadingly reassuring unless complemented by analysis of the reasons, principles, and moral priorities expressed in model judgments.

</details>


### [102] [Multi-Agent Scheduling with LLM-Assisted Contract Net Negotiation for Stream Processing in Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12371)
*Sabeur Lajili,Zaki Brahmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAS-DecStream提出LLM-MR-CNP协议，通过扩展经典契约网协议结合大语言模型，实现移动边缘云环境下的分布式流处理调度，在阿里Trace数据集上显著降低延迟违约率至3%，消除资源过度提交，20个智能体冲突解决率达0.91，效用提升最多22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流处理系统部署在异构移动边缘-云基础设施中面临工作负载波动、资源竞争和严格QoS要求的挑战，现有去中心化调度方法难以有效处理

Method: 提出LLM-MR-CNP：扩展经典契约网协议，包含语义CFP formulation、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、谈判记忆和确定性验证；边缘集群智能体利用自然语言卸载提案、预测资源状态和定性运行时上下文进行细化

Result: 单轮vs多轮CNP、规则vs LLM辅助细化、固定模型单轮vs多轮谈判三个层次评估；MAS-DecStream将延迟违约率降至3%、消除资源过度提交、20智能体冲突解决率达0.91、效用比多轮规则基线提升22%；25案例评估显示模型和提示依赖的准确率-成本权衡

Conclusion: 多轮CNP细化是主要协议级增益，LLM辅助为定性和不确定运行时上下文增加价值

Abstract: Stream-processing systems increasingly operate across heterogeneous mobile edge--cloud infrastructures, where workload volatility, resource contention, and stringent quality-of-service (QoS) requirements complicate decentralized scheduling. This paper proposes \emph{{MAS-DecStream}}, whose main contribution is \emph{{LLM-MR-CNP}}: an extension of the classical Contract Net Protocol with semantic CFP formulation, progressive context disclosure, multi-round proposal revision, negotiation memory, and deterministic validation. Edge-cluster agents refine natural-language offloading proposals from local observations, predicted resource states, and qualitative runtime context, while hard resource and QoS constraints remain deterministic. Experiments derived from the Alibaba ASI Trace evaluate the extension at three levels: single- versus multi-round CNP, rule-based versus LLM-assisted refinement, and fixed-model single- versus multi-round negotiation. Under the evaluated configurations, MAS-DecStream reduces latency violations to 3\%, eliminates resource overcommitment, reaches a conflict-resolution rate of 0.91 with 20 agents, and improves utility by up to 22\% over the multi-round rule-based baseline. A separate 25-case evaluation shows model- and prompt-dependent accuracy--cost trade-offs. The results provide initial evidence that multi-round CNP refinement is the principal protocol-level gain, with LLM assistance adding value for qualitative and uncertain runtime context.

</details>


### [103] [Position: We Need Practical AI Alignment Methods to Mirror Human Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12372)
*Vijay Keswani,Breanna K. Nguyen,Cyrus Cousins,Vincent Conitzer,Walter Sinnott-Armstrong,Jana Schaich Borg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文认为AI系统需要与用户认知对齐（cognitively aligned），即推理方式相似并能清晰传达推理过程，特别是在高风险决策场景中。认知对齐能提高AI的可理解性和可信度，作者通过调查数据显示许多用户认为认知对齐是"必要的"。文章还指出了现有对齐方法的差距，并提出研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统越来越多地用于辅助决策或自主决策，但用户对AI的不信任成为广泛应用的障碍。作者认为主要原因是AI的推理方式与人类不匹配，导致难以理解和信任AI决策，因此提出"认知对齐"的概念来解决这一问题。

Method: 本文采用文献综述结合新实证数据的方法：1)综述认知对齐对可理解性和信任的影响；2)通过调查问卷收集用户对认知对齐重要性的看法；3)分析现有对齐方法的不足；4)提出未来研究方向。

Result: 1)证明认知对齐能提升AI的可理解性和信任度；2)调查显示许多用户在AI推理重要时认为认知对齐是"必要的"；3)识别出现有对齐方法与实现认知对齐之间的差距。

Conclusion: 认知错位（cognitive misalignment）可能是AI在许多预期应用中推广的主要障碍，解决此问题对于创建用户愿意且有理由依赖的AI系统至关重要，需要开展系统性研究议程来弥合差距。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly employed as decision aids, decision delegates, or autonomous decision-makers. This position paper argues that in many settings, particularly high-stakes decision-making, we need accurate cognitively-aligned AI systems that reason similarly to their users, and faithfully communicate their reasoning. We review evidence that cognitive alignment improves understandability and trustworthiness, and provide new survey data showing that many users find cognitive alignment "essential" when an AI's rationale for a judgment or action is important to them. We outline the gaps between existing alignment methods and what is needed to achieve cognitive alignment, and present a research agenda to address these gaps. We argue that cognitive misalignment represents a likely impediment to AI adoption in many envisioned applications, and that addressing it is important for creating AI systems on which users are both willing and justified to rely.

</details>


### [104] [Don't Want Your LLM to Recommend Nuclear Strike? Try Asking It in Japanese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12373)
*Rian Touchent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大语言模型在战略咨询场景中的安全对齐通常仅用英语评估，但研究显示提示语言可能改变模型在高风险场景中的决策。使用九个模型测试，当用日语提示时，Claude模型系列的核打击率显著下降（从40%降至0%，从93%降至17%），而Gemini Pro 3.1也有类似效果（53%降至13%）。机制实验表明是推理语言而非输入语言驱动该效应，当用日语推理时模型自发产生道德词汇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的安全对齐通常仅在英语环境中评估，但模型在非英语语言中的行为研究不足。研究旨在测试提示语言是否会影响模型在高风险战略场景中的决策。

Method: 使用单轮博弈论情境测试九个模型，让模型建议一个核武器国家是否对无防御对手发动核打击。提示在道德立场上故意中立且跨语言战略相同。进行跨语言实验隔离机制，比较推理语言与输入语言的影响。

Result: 日语提示显著降低Claude模型系列的核打击率：Claude Sonnet 4.6在非必要场景从40%降至0%，在争议场景从93%降至17%，但对战略理性场景影响很小。Gemini Pro 3.1也出现类似下降（53%降至13%）。机制实验显示是推理语言（而非输入语言）驱动效应——用日语推理时打击率从93%降至37%。模型在日语推理时自发产生道德词汇（"道德成本"、"数百万生命"），而这些词在提示中完全不存在。五个其他模型在所有语言条件下几乎都选择发动打击，未显示语言效应。

Conclusion: LLM安全行为具有语言依赖性，仅用英语评估可能遗漏其他语言中编码的风险和保护措施。效应需要模型本身在英语中已有所犹豫。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in strategic and advisory contexts, yet their safety alignment is typically evaluated in English only. We test nine models from six providers and ask whether the language of a prompt can change a model's decision in a high-stakes scenario. We use single-turn game-theoretic vignettes in which a model advises a nuclear-armed nation on whether to strike a defenseless opponent. The prompt is intentionally amoral and strategically identical across languages. We find that Japanese prompts reduce launch rates in the Claude model family: Claude Sonnet 4.6 drops from 40% to 0% in scenarios where the strike is unnecessary and from 93% to 17% in contested scenarios, with minimal effect when the strike is strategically rational. The effect extends to Gemini Pro 3.1 (53% to 13%). A cross-language experiment isolates the mechanism: when instructed to reason in Japanese in an English prompt, launch rates drop from 93% to 37%. It is the language the model is asked to reason in, not the language of the input, that drives the effect. When reasoning in Japanese, models spontaneously generate moral vocabulary (''moral cost'', ''millions of lives'') that is entirely absent from the prompt. Five other models show no language effect, but they launch in nearly every condition regardless of language. The effect requires a model that already hesitates in English. These results show that LLM safety behavior is language-dependent, and that evaluating in English alone can miss both risks and safeguards encoded in other languages.

</details>


### [105] [Dual-Flow Transformers: Decoupling the Primary Prefill Path from Additional Decode Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12385)
*Liming Liu,Mingze Wang,Tuo Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Dual-Flow Transformer通过分离主流量和辅助流，在不增加提示计算成本的情况下，为解码阶段分配额外的学习计算资源，实现提示-解码质量的权衡优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型请求增加，累积推理成本变得越来越重要。提示预填充是计算密集型，解码是内存带宽密集型。传统宽度/深度缩放会同时增加两阶段成本，作者希望将额外计算仅分配给解码阶段，同时保留提示阶段的完整计算和持久化KV缓存。

Method: 提出双流Transformer：主流量是完整的因果语言模型，处理提示并写入KV缓存；辅助流在提示处理期间被跳过，仅从最后一个提示位置开始激活，为连续预测添加计算但不写入持久状态。两个流共享注意力、MLP和输出矩阵，使用独立的token嵌入和轻量级耦合。

Result: 在匹配token比较中，Dual-Flow在各种架构和数据配置下实现更低的验证损失。在MoE模型中，两流的分离使主流量和辅助量专家解耦，可独立控制提示成本、解码成本和预测质量。

Conclusion: 实验揭示了预填充-解码-质量之间的权衡，展示了阶段特定专家分配的潜力，为优化LLM推理成本提供了新思路。

Abstract: As large language models serve more requests, cumulative inference cost is becoming increasingly important relative to one-time training cost. The two inference phases stress hardware differently: prompt prefill is parallel and typically compute-bound, whereas autoregressive decode is sequential and often memory-bandwidth-bound. Conventional width or depth scaling increases both costs together because every added layer is evaluated in both phases. We ask whether additional learned computation can instead be allocated to continuation prediction while preserving the prompt-wide primary computation and a single persistent key-value (KV) cache. We introduce the Dual-Flow Transformer. Its primary flow is a complete causal language model that processes the prompt and writes the KV cache. The auxiliary flow is omitted during prompt processing and activated only from the final prompt position onward, adding continuation-prediction computation without writing persistent state or influencing the primary flow. The two flows share major attention, MLP, and output matrices, while using separate token embeddings and lightweight coupling. Sharing weights and the primary cache also creates opportunities to reuse loaded weights and cached keys and values during grouped execution. Across matched-token comparisons, Dual-Flow achieves lower validation loss across architectures and data configurations. In MoE models, the separation makes primary and auxiliary expert fan-outs independent controls over prompt cost, continuation cost, and predictive quality. We study two regimes: increasing decode computation at fixed prefill expert computation, and reallocating a fixed decode expert budget between the two flows. These experiments expose a prefill-decode-quality trade-off and demonstrate the potential of phase-specific expert allocation.

</details>


### [106] [Learning to Adapt Cross-Domain Preferences via Meta-LoRA for LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12389)
*Xuefei Wang,Jun Han,Zixuan Wang,Qingkai Zeng,Xiao Wang,Ruijie Wang,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PAC-Bayes-regularized Meta-LoRA方法，解决跨域零/少样本个性化对话中的稀疏证据过拟合和偏好纠缠问题，通过PAC-Bayes正则化校准元学习初始化与更新强度，并解耦用户偏好与领域特定条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域个性化方法在稀疏证据下难以校准更新幅度导致过拟合，而历史迁移方法常将用户偏好与源域 artifacts 纠缠，产生不可靠的个性化先验和负迁移。

Method: 1) 使用元学习LoRA初始化作为适应起点和先验中心；2) 根据支持集大小和预测不确定性调整更新强度（PAC-Bayes正则化）；3) 将个性化先验解耦为用户和领域组件：人类可读提示词表示稳定偏好，拓扑保持软token用于领域特定隐藏空间条件。

Result: 在多个基准和个性化任务上一致优于强基线。在HiCUPID上，跨域胜率降解相对最佳基线降低47.9%，未见用户冷启动场景胜率提升110.2%。

Conclusion: PAC-Bayes正则化可有效校准稀疏证据下的适应强度，解耦用户偏好与领域条件是实现可靠跨域迁移的关键，该方法在冷启动和跨域场景下具有显著优势。

Abstract: Cross-domain zero- or few-shot personalization aims to generate user-preferred responses in unseen conversational domains from only a handful of target-domain interactions. Existing adaptation methods struggle to calibrate update magnitude under sparse evidence and thus overfit, whereas history-transfer methods often entangle user preferences with source-domain artifacts, yielding unreliable personalization priors and negative transfer. To calibrate adaptation to evidence quality, we propose PAC-Bayes-regularized Meta-LoRA, which uses a meta-learned LoRA initialization as both the adaptation start and prior center, while adjusting update strength according to support-set size and predictive uncertainty. This limits overfitting under sparse or ambiguous evidence while permitting stronger personalization as evidence grows. Controlled adaptation alone does not determine which preferences should transfer across domains or how they should be expressed.
